- **geniro-actions**: Use when creating or running a reusable workflow helper (Slack/PR/release automation) in .geniro/actions/; running one is the authorization, no confirm step. Skip for editing core Geniro skills. ([geniro-io/geniro-claude-harness](https://github.com/geniro-io/geniro-claude-harness/tree/HEAD/cursor/skills/geniro-actions) · ★8)
- **geniro-audit-instructions**: Use when auditing the repo's AI-instruction files (CLAUDE.md, AGENTS.md, Cursor/Copilot rules) for staleness, drift, bloat, or gaps. Skip for generating CLAUDE.md (/geniro:setup), one rule (/geniro:instructions), or session mining (/geniro:reflect). ([geniro-io/geniro-claude-harness](https://github.com/geniro-io/geniro-claude-harness/tree/HEAD/cursor/skills/geniro-audit-instructions) · ★8)
- **geniro-debug**: Use when a bug needs systematic root-cause investigation: hypothesize, test, isolate, reproduce, then hand the fix to /geniro:implement. Adversarial mode (verify-changes) writes failing tests for a diff. Skip for obvious causes (/geniro:implement). ([geniro-io/geniro-claude-harness](https://github.com/geniro-io/geniro-claude-harness/tree/HEAD/cursor/skills/geniro-debug) · ★8)
- **geniro-implement**: Use when shipping a new feature, endpoint, page, or significant change against a spec.md / plan.md (from /geniro:plan) OR a raw inline task description. 3-phase autonomous loop: Analyze → Implement → Self-review-and-Ship. ([geniro-io/geniro-claude-harness](https://github.com/geniro-io/geniro-claude-harness/tree/HEAD/cursor/skills/geniro-implement) · ★8)
- **geniro-instructions**: Use when managing Geniro project rules: phase-boundary skill rules, code-style rules, Data Sources, Verification Surface, or a Memory Backend (e.g. an MCP). Ops: list/create/edit/validate/delete. Skip for per-file-pattern rules (.claude/rules/). ([geniro-io/geniro-claude-harness](https://github.com/geniro-io/geniro-claude-harness/tree/HEAD/cursor/skills/geniro-instructions) · ★8)
- **geniro-investigate**: Use when a deep codebase question needs an evidence-backed answer from code, git history, or the web; parallel research agents return cited answers. Skip for bug fixes (/geniro:debug) or codebase mapping (/geniro:onboard). ([geniro-io/geniro-claude-harness](https://github.com/geniro-io/geniro-claude-harness/tree/HEAD/cursor/skills/geniro-investigate) · ★8)
- **geniro-onboard**: Use when orienting in an unfamiliar codebase: scans structure and conventions into _CODEBASE_MAP.md (architecture, modules, critical paths, entry points). Skip for specific questions (/geniro:investigate) or bugs (/geniro:debug). ([geniro-io/geniro-claude-harness](https://github.com/geniro-io/geniro-claude-harness/tree/HEAD/cursor/skills/geniro-onboard) · ★8)
- **geniro-plan**: Use when turning a vague idea or feature request into an approved spec.md before /geniro:implement: explore, grill, compare approaches, approve. --artifact adds a live visual plan. Skip when a spec exists (/geniro:implement {path}). ([geniro-io/geniro-claude-harness](https://github.com/geniro-io/geniro-claude-harness/tree/HEAD/cursor/skills/geniro-plan) · ★8)
- **geniro-refactor**: Use when restructuring code or paying down tech debt with zero behavior change; the working-tree diff is the deliverable, never shipped. Skip for behavior changes (/geniro:implement) or performance (/geniro:review). ([geniro-io/geniro-claude-harness](https://github.com/geniro-io/geniro-claude-harness/tree/HEAD/cursor/skills/geniro-refactor) · ★8)
- **geniro-reflect**: Use when the user wants project rules learned from past sessions or this one: corrections, rejected suggestions, friction. Takes a search string, --this-session, or nothing. Skip for code questions (/geniro:investigate) or diffs (/geniro:review). ([geniro-io/geniro-claude-harness](https://github.com/geniro-io/geniro-claude-harness/tree/HEAD/cursor/skills/geniro-reflect) · ★8)
- **geniro-resolve**: Use when an open PR has unresolved review comments (human or bot) or failing CI to handle: triages, pushes back with evidence, applies accepted fixes, replies, resolves threads. Skip for a fresh diff (/geniro:review) or a bug with no PR feedback. ([geniro-io/geniro-claude-harness](https://github.com/geniro-io/geniro-claude-harness/tree/HEAD/cursor/skills/geniro-resolve) · ★8)
- **geniro-review**: Use when pending changes (a diff, branch, or PR) need a code review. Parallel per-dimension reviewers; verified findings land in a handoff for /geniro:implement. Never edits code; asks before posting to a PR. ([geniro-io/geniro-claude-harness](https://github.com/geniro-io/geniro-claude-harness/tree/HEAD/cursor/skills/geniro-review) · ★8)
- **geniro-setup**: Use when starting on a new codebase or after a major plugin update. Detects tech stack, generates a project-specific CLAUDE.md (stack, commands, conventions, domain), and validates it. Re-run mode runs a migration sweep. Singleton bootstrap. ([geniro-io/geniro-claude-harness](https://github.com/geniro-io/geniro-claude-harness/tree/HEAD/cursor/skills/geniro-setup) · ★8)
- **geniro-update**: Use when the status line shows a Geniro update or the user asks to update the plugin. Verifies integrity, checks .geniro/instructions/ and .geniro/actions/ survived, and walks MIGRATION.md breaking changes. ([geniro-io/geniro-claude-harness](https://github.com/geniro-io/geniro-claude-harness/tree/HEAD/cursor/skills/geniro-update) · ★8)
- **genitourinary**: Use when reasoning about genitourinary as a schema.org vocabulary word — the single word collided from the schema.org terms that contain it, content-addressed into the corpus. ([erpax/erpax](https://github.com/erpax/erpax/tree/HEAD/src/vocabulary/genitourinary) · ★2)
- **genius**: The map of the Working Genius workflow — where each piece of work stands, what to run next, and where genius gaps are hiding. ([donald-ada/workinggenius](https://github.com/donald-ada/workinggenius/tree/HEAD/skills/genius) · ★6)
- **genius**: Use when the user runs /genius or asks to run the GENIUS multi-agent idea→portfolio pipeline. Computes the run directory and launches the bundled debate-engine workflow. ([chenpg2/genius-plugin](https://github.com/chenpg2/genius-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/genius) · ★2)
- **genius**: A thinking partner that is smarter, not just more thorough. Maps problems before committing, challenges premises, draws cross-domain insights, and follows your direction while surfacing what you haven't considered. ([EndUser123/cc-skills-meta](https://github.com/EndUser123/cc-skills-meta/tree/HEAD/skills/genius))
- **genius-evolve**: Use when the user runs /genius-evolve or asks to run the EVOLVE engine on an already-prepared evolve-dir (initial_program.py + frozen evaluator.py + frozen.lock). ([chenpg2/genius-plugin](https://github.com/chenpg2/genius-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/genius-evolve) · ★2)
- **genius-file**: Read and update Working Genius work files — the work's folder holding the bounded snapshot a cold session acts on, the append-only log behind it, and the contract that binds the unbuilt slices. Use when a stage skill needs the work-file discipline, when the user asks what work is in flight or where a piece of work left off, or before resuming any work tracked under .genius/. ([donald-ada/workinggenius](https://github.com/donald-ada/workinggenius/tree/HEAD/skills/genius-file) · ★6)
- **genius-focus**: Use when the user runs /genius-focus or asks GENIUS to break a local sub-problem (a principle/formula/math/method) inside an EXISTING project. Auto-understands the project, surfaces candidate sub-problems, user picks one, then runs a scoped axioms-grounded debate (+gated EVOLVE). ([chenpg2/genius-plugin](https://github.com/chenpg2/genius-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/genius-focus) · ★2)
- **genius-life**: Guide a person in cultivating creativity in their own work and life: open conversational sessions on creative blocks, habits, environment, motivation, and resilience, or structured development of a concrete project or fledgling idea through a five-phase practice. Do not use for therapy or clinical support, general life coaching, product or stakeholder discovery, or as a study guide for a book. ([magnus919/agent-skills](https://github.com/magnus919/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/genius-life) · ★85)
- **genius-thinking**: A structured deep-reasoning protocol distilled from the documented working methods of history's most exceptional thinkers (Einstein, Feynman, Darwin, Shannon, Poincaré, von Neumann) and from cognitive-science research on creative breakthrough. It replaces the default "first plausible answer" with forced problem interrogation, divergent generation, adversarial selection, and severe testing. Use this skill whenever the user asks for breakthrough or novel ideas, clever/elegant/non-obvious solutions, new algorithms or approaches, research-level problem solving, invention, "think harder", "think l… ([Harduex/skills](https://github.com/Harduex/skills/tree/HEAD/genius-thinking))
- **genius-understand**: Use when the user runs /genius-understand or asks GENIUS to map/diagnose the current project. Runs the bundled understand workflow and presents the grounded map. Persists understand-map.md and understand-map.json to the runDir immediately after the workflow returns. ([chenpg2/genius-plugin](https://github.com/chenpg2/genius-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/genius-understand) · ★2)
